  m = model\\\\n    for part in parts:\\\\n        m = getattr(m, part)\\\\n    return m\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass LoRAInjector:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Load LoRA adapters and inject/restore weight deltas.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, model, device):\\\\n        self.model = model\\\\n        self.device = device\\\\n        self.experts = {}\\\\n        self.scaling = 2.0  # alpha/rank = 32/16\\\\n        self._frozen_weights = {}\\\\n\\\\n    def load_adapter(self, name, adapter_dir):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Load PEFT adapter from checkpoint directory.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        safetensors_path = os.path.join(adapter_dir, \\\\\\\"adapter_model.safetensors\\\\\\\")\\\\n        state_dict = safetensors.torch.load_file(safetensors_path, device=str(self.device))\\\\n\\\\n        expert_params = {}\\\\n        for key, tensor in state_dict.items():\\\\n            if \\\\\\\"lora_A\\\\\\\" in key:\\\\n                module_name = key.replace(\\\\\\\"base_model.model.\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\").split(\\\\\\\".lora_A\\\\\\\")[0]\\\\n                expert_params.setdefault(module_name, {})[\\\\\\\"lora_A\\\\\\\"] = tensor.float()\\\\n            elif \\\\\\\"lora_B\\\\\\\" in key:\\\\n                module_name = key.replace(\\\\\\\"base_model.model.\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\").split(\\\\\\\".lora_B\\\\\\\")[0]\\\\n                expert_params.setdefault(module_name, {})[\\\\\\\"lora_B\\\\\\\"] = tensor.float()\\\\n\\\\n        self.experts[name] = expert_params\\\\n\\\\n        config_path = os.path.join(adapter_dir, \\\\\\\"adapter_config.json\\\\\\\")\\\\n        if os.path.exists(config_path):\\\\n            with open(config_path) as f:\\\\n                cfg = json.load(f)\\\\n            self.scaling = cfg.get(\\\\\\\"lora_alpha\\\\\\\", 32) / cfg.get(\\\\\\\"r\\\\\\\", 16)\\\\n\\\\n        log.info(\\\\\\\"Loaded LoRA '%s': %d modules (scaling=%.1f)\\\\\\\", name, len(expert_params), self.scaling)\\\\n\\\\n    def _save_frozen_weights(self):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Save original weights for first-use modules.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if self._frozen_weights:\\\\n            return\\\\n        ref = next(iter(self.experts.values()))\\\\n        for module_path in ref:\\\\n            mod = _get_submodule(self.model, module_path)\\\\n            self._frozen_weights[module_path] = mod.weight.data.clone()\\\\n\\\\n    def inject(self, routing_weights=None, expert_name=None):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Inject LoRA deltas into model weights.\\\\n\\\\n        Either:\\\\n        - expert_name: inject single expert with weight=1.0\\\\n        - routing_weights: [N] tensor, blend N experts\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        self._save_frozen_weights()\\\\n\\\\n        if expert_name is not None:\\\\n            params = self.experts[expert_name]\\\\n            for module_path, lora in params.items():\\\\n                A = lora[\\\\\\\"lora_A\\\\\\\"]  # [rank, in_features]\\\\n                B = lora[\\\\\\\"lora_B\\\\\\\"]  # [out_features, rank]\\\\n                delta = self.scaling * (B @ A)\\\\n                mod = _get_submodule(self.model, module_path)\\\\n                mod.weight.data.add_(delta.to(mod.weight.dtype))\\\\n        elif routing_weights is not None:\\\\n            expert_names = list(self.experts.keys())\\\\n            ref = self.experts[expert_names[0]]\\\\n            for module_path in ref:\\\\n                merged_A = torch.zeros_like(ref[module_path][\\\\\\\"lora_A\\\\\\\"])\\\\n                merged_B = torch.zeros_like(ref[module_path][\\\\\\\"lora_B\\\\\\\"])\\\\n                for i, en in enumerate(expert_names):\\\\n                    merged_A += routing_weights[i] * self.experts[en][module_path][\\\\\\\"lora_A\\\\\\\"]\\\\n                    merged_B += routing_weights[i] * self.experts[en][module_path][\\\\\\\"lora_B\\\\\\\"]\\\\n                delta = self.scaling * (merged_B @ merged_A)\\\\n                mod = _get_submodule(self.model, module_path)\\\\n                mod.weight.data.add_(delta.to(mod.weight.dtype))\\\\n\\\\n    def restore(self):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Restore frozen weights.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        for module_path, frozen_w in self._frozen_weights.items():\\\\n            mod = _get_submodule(self.model, module_path)\\\\n            mod.weight.data.copy_(frozen_w)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n# PAR Router (lightweight)\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n\\\\nclass PARRouterLite:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Simplified PAR router for eval: hooks layer 6, runs classifier.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, checkpoint_path, model, device):\\\\n        from par_router import PARRouter\\\\n        self.router = layer_mod.register_forward_hook(self._capture_hook)\\\\n\\\\n    def _capture_hook(self, module, input, output):\\\\n        if isinstance(output, tuple):\\\\n            self._hidden = output[0].detach()\\\\n        else:\\\\n            self._hidden = output.detach()\\\\n\\\\n    def get_routing_weights(self):\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Return routing weights from last captured hidden states.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if self._hidden is None:\\\\n            return torch.tensor([1/3, 1/3, 1/3], device=self.device)\\\\n        weights = NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\\\\n    LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION,\\\\n    IMAGE_SIZE,\\\\n)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n# Main eval\\\\n# import PARRouter\\n        self.router = PARRouter.from_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, device=device)\\n        self.model = model\\n        self.device = device\\n        self._hidden = None\\n\\n self._hook = layer_mod.register_forward_hook(self._capture_hook)\\n\\n    def _capture_hook(self, module, input, output):\\n        if isinstance(output, tuple):\\n            self._hidden = output[0].detach()\\n        else:\\n            self._hidden = output.detach()\\n\\n    def get_routing_weights(self):\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Return routing weights from last captured hidden states.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if self._hidden is None:\\n            return torch.tensor([1/3, 1/3, 1/3], device=self.device)\\n        weights = self.router.route(self._hidden)\\n        return weights[0]  # [3]\\n\\n    def cleanup(self):\\n        self._hook.remove()\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Eval environment (reuse from REPLAN_STEPS,\\n    NUM_STEPS_WAIT,\\n    LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION,\\n    IMAGE_SIZE,\\n)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Main eval\\n# \\\"def get_tasks_by_perturbation(task_suite, perturbation_type):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Return task identifiers for the given (suite, perturbation_type).\\\\n\\\\n    For 'nominal': returns synthetic negative indices that map back to base\\\\n    LIBERO BDDL files via _nominal_index_to_name().\\\\n    For perturbation categories: returns task name strings from\\\\n    task_classification.json (used directly by create_libero_env).\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if perturbation_type == \\\\\\\"nominal\\\\\\\":\\\\n        names = _list_nominal_task_names(task_suite)\\\\n        return [-(i + 1) for i in range(len(names))]\\\\n\\\\n    cat = PERTURBATION_TO_JSON_CATEGORY.get(perturbation_type)\\\\n    if cat is None:\\\\n        raise ValueError(\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"Unknown perturbation_type {perturbation_type!r}. \\\\\\\"\\\\n            f\\\\\\\"Valid: {sorted(PERTURBATION_TO_JSON_CATEGORY)} or 'nominal'.\\\\\\\"\\\\n        )\\\\n    tc = _load_task_classification()\\\\n    target_names = [it[\\\\\\\"name\\\\\\\"] for it in tc.get(task_suite, []) if it[\\\\\\\"category\\\\\\\"] == cat]\\\\n    return target_names\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _nominal_index_to_name(task_suite, neg_idx):\\\\n    names = _list_nominal_task_names(task_suite)\\\\n    pos = -neg_idx - 1\\\\n    if pos < 0 or pos >= len(names):\\\\n        raise IndexError(f\\\\\\\"Nominal index {neg_idx} out of range for {task_suite}\\\\\\\")\\\\n    return names[pos]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n# Observation preprocessing (from libero_integration.py)\\\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n\\\\ndef _quat2axisangle(quat):\\\\n    q = np.asarray(quat, dtype=np.float64)\\\\n    norm = np.linalg.norm(q)\\\\n    if norm < 1e-8:\\\\n        return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\\\\n    q /= norm\\\\n    sin_half = np.linalg.norm(q[:3])\\\\n    if sin_half < 1e-8:\\\\n        return np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32)\\\\n    angle = 2.0 * math.atan2(sin_half, q[3])\\\\n    axis = q[:3] / sin_half\\\\n    return (axis * angle).astype(np.float32)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef _resize_with_pad(img, size):\\\\n    from PIL import Image as PILImage\\\\n    h, w = img.shape[:2]\\\\n    scale = min(size / h, size / w)\\\\n    new_h, new_w = int(h * scale), int(w * scale)\\\\n    resized = np.array(\\\\n        PILImage.fromarray(img).resize((new_w, new_h), PILImage.BILINEAR)\\\\n    )\\\\n    canvas = np.zeros((size, size, 3), dtype=np.uint8)\\\\n    y0, x0 = (size - new_h) // 2, (size - new_w) // 2\\\\n    canvas[y0 : y0 + new_h, x0 : x0 + new_w] = resized\\\\n    return canvas\\\\n\\\\n\\\\ndef obs_to_model_input(obs, task_description):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Convert LIBERO env observation to dict for MetaBrain.infer().\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    agentview = np.ascontiguousarray(obs[\\\\\\\"agentview_image\\\\\\\"][::-1])\\\\n    wrist = np.ascontiguousarray(obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\\\\\\\"][::-1])\\\\n\\\\n    if agentview.shape[:2] != (MODEL_INPUT_SIZE, MODEL_INPUT_SIZE):\\\\n        agentview = _resize_with_pad(agentview, MODEL_INPUT_SIZE)\\\\n    if wrist.shape[:2] != (MODEL_INPUT_SIZE, MODEL_INPUT_SIZE):\\\\n        wrist = _resize_with_pad(wrist, MODEL_INPUT_SIZE)\\\\n\\\\n    state = np.concatenate([\\\\n        obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_eef_pos\\\\\\\"],\\\\n        _quat2axisangle(obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_eef_quat\\\\\\\"]),\\\\n        obs[\\\\\\\"robot0_gripper_qpos\\\\\\\"],\\\\n    ])\\\\n\\\\n    return {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation/image\\\\\\\": agentview,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation/wrist_image\\\\\\\": wrist,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"observation/state\\\\\\\": \\\"def run_episode(\\\\n    meta_brain,\\\\n    task_suite_name,\\\\n    perturbation_type,\\\\n    seed,\\\\n    max_steps=None,\\\\n):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Run one LIBERO-Plus episode with MetaBrain.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if max_steps is None:\\\\n        max_steps = MAX_STEPS.get(task_suite_name, 400)\\\\n\\\\n    env, task_description, init_states, task_idx, suite_key = create_libero_env(\\\\n        task_suite_name, perturbation_type, seed,\\\\n    )\\\\n\\\\n    import random\\\\n    trial_idx = random.randint(0, len(init_states) - 1)\\\\n\\\\n    step = 0\\\\n    success = False\\\\n    episode_routing = []\\\\n\\\\n    try:\\\\n        obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[trial_idx])\\\\n        for _ in range(NUM_STEPS_WAIT):\\\\n            obs, _, _, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\\\\n\\\\n        action_plan = collections.deque()\\\\n        done = False\\\\n\\\\n        while not done and step < max_steps:\\\\n            if len(action_plan) == 0:\\\\n                obs_dict = obs_to_model_input(obs, task_description)\\\\n                result = meta_brain.infer(obs_dict)\\\\n                action_chunk = result[\\\\\\\"actions\\\\\\\"]\\\\n                action_plan.extend(action_chunk[:REPLAN_STEPS])\\\\n                if \\\\\\\"routing_weights\\\\\\\" in result:\\\\n                    episode_routing.append(result[\\\\\\\"routing_weights\\\\\\\"])\\\\n\\\\n            action = action_plan.popleft()\\\\n            if hasattr(action, \\\\\\\"tolist\\\\\\\"):\\\\n                action = action.tolist()\\\\n            obs, reward, done, info = env.step(action)\\\\n            step += 1\\\\n\\\\n            if done or reward > 0:\\\\n                success = info.get(\\\\\\\"success\\\\\\\", reward > 0)\\\\n    finally:\\\\n        env.close()\\\\n\\\\n    return {\\\\n        \\\\\\\"success\\\\\\\": success,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"steps\\\\\\\": step,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"task_description\\\\\\\": task_description,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"task_idx\\\\\\\": task_idx,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"suite_key\\\\\\\": suite_key,\\\\n        \\\\\\\"perturbation_type\\\\\\\": perturbation_type or \\\\\\\"nominal\\\\\\\",\\\\n        \\\\\\\"routing_weights_mean\\\\\\\": (\\\\n            np.mean(episode_routing, axis=0).tolist() if ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\\\n\\\\ndef evaluate(\\\\n    meta_brain,\\\\n    perturbation_types,\\\\n    task_suite_name=\\\\\\\"libero_spatial\\\\\\\",\\\\n    num_episodes=50,\\\\n    seeds=None,\\\\n):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Evaluate MetaBrain across perturbation types and seeds.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n    if seeds is None:\\\\n        seeds = [42]\\\\n\\\\n    results = {}\\\\n    for ptype in perturbation_types:\\\\n        log.info(\\\\\\\"Evaluating {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_0143K8sgXiaNXpr8hEz8Syrj","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01S7RvCA5TLDNSYJKdBs2pRF","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-34142","command":"cat > /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/collect_activations_real_env.py << 'SCRIPT'\n\"\"\"\nCollect PaliGemma layer-6 activations from REAL LIBERO-Plus environments.\n\nUnlike v3 (synthetic perturbations on clean images), this script:\n1. Loads actual LIBERO-Plus perturbation environments (background_textures, \n   camera_viewpoints, light_conditions, sensor_noise, objects_layout, robot_initial_states)\n2. Resets envs, steps to get real perturbed observations\n3. Runs pi0 model forward pass\n4. Captures layer-6 first-token activations\n\nOutput: /root/autodl-tmp/activations_real_env/activations.pt\n\"\"\"\n\nimport os\nos.environ[\"JAXTYPING_DISABLE\"] = \"1\"\nos.environ[\"MUJOCO_GL\"] = \"egl\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport collections\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport random\nimport sys\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\n\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase1\")\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/root/meta_brain_lora_routing\")\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/root/openpi/src\")\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/root/openpi\")\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\")\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nPERTURBATION_TO_3CLASS = {\n    \"camera_viewpoints\": \"visual\",\n    \"light_conditions\": \"visual\",\n    \"background_textures\": \"visual\",\n    \"sensor_noise\": \"visual\",\n    \"objects_layout\": \"spatial\",\n    \"robot_initial_states\": \"physical\",\n}\n\nCLASS_TO_IDX = {\"visual\": 0, \"spatial\": 1, \"physical\": 2}\n\n\nclass Layer6Collector:\n    def __init__(self, model):\n        self.model = model\n        self._hook = None\n        self._activation = None\n\n    def register(self):\n        module_path = \"paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.model.language_model.layers.6\"\n        m = self.model\n        for p in module_path.split(\".\"):\n            m = getattr(m, p)\n        self._hook = m.register_forward_hook(self._hook_fn)\n        log.info(\"Hooked PaliGemma layer 6 (id=%d)\", id(m))\n\n    def _hook_fn(self, _module, _input, output):\n        if isinstance(output, tuple):\n            h = output[0]\n        else:\n            h = output\n        self._activation = h[:, 0].detach().float().cpu()\n\n    def get(self):\n        act = self._activation\n        self._activation = None\n        return act\n\n    def remove(self):\n        if self._hook:\n            self._hook.remove()\n            self._hook = None\n\n\ndef collect_from_perturbation(policy, collector, task_suite, perturbation_type,\n                              num_tasks, steps_per_task, seed):\n    \"\"\"Collect activations from one perturbation type.\"\"\"\n    from evaluate_libero_plus import (\n        get_tasks_by_perturbation, create_libero_env, obs_to_model_input,\n        NUM_STEPS_WAIT, LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION,\n    )\n\n    task_ids = get_tasks_by_perturbation(task_suite, perturbation_type)\n    if not task_ids:\n        log.warning(\"No tasks for %s/%s\", task_suite, perturbation_type)\n        return [], []\n\n    rng = random.Random(seed)\n    selected_tasks = rng.sample(task_ids, min(num_tasks, len(task_ids)))\n    \n    activations = []\n    metadata = []\n    \n    for task_idx, task_id in enumerate(selected_tasks):\n        try:\n            env, task_desc, init_states, sel_idx, suite_key = create_libero_env(\n                task_suite, perturbation_type, seed + task_idx\n            )\n        except Exception as e:\n            log.warning(\"Failed to create env for task %s: %s\", task_id, e)\n            continue\n\n        try:\n            trial_idx = rng.randint(0, len(init_states) - 1)\n            obs = env.set_init_state(init_states[trial_idx])\n            for _ in range(NUM_STEPS_WAIT):\n                obs, _, _, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n\n            for step in range(steps_per_task):\n                obs_dict = obs_to_model_input(obs, task_desc)\n                _ = policy.infer(obs_dict)\n                act = collector.get()\n                \n                if act is not None:\n                    activations.append(act.squeeze(0))\n                    metadata.append({\n                        \"perturbation_type\": perturbation_type,\n                        \"class_3\": PERTURBATION_TO_3CLASS[perturbation_type],\n                        \"task_suite\": task_suite,\n                        \"task_id\": str(sel_idx),\n                        \"step\": step,\n                    })\n                \n                obs, _, done, _ = env.step(LIBERO_DUMMY_ACTION)\n                if done:\n                    break\n        except Exception as e:\n            log.warning(\"Error during collection from %s task %d: %s\", \n                       perturbation_type, task_idx, e)\n        finally:\n            env.close()\n        \n        if (task_idx + 1) % 10 == 0:\n            log.info(\"  [%s] %d/%d tasks done, %d activations collected\",\n                    perturbation_type, task_idx + 1, len(selected_tasks), len(activations))\n\n    return activations, metadata\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--device\", default=\"cuda:2\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/activations_real_env\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--task_suite\", default=\"libero_spatial\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--num_tasks_per_perturbation\", type=int, default=50)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--steps_per_task\", type=int, default=10)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--seed\", type=int, default=42)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--checkpoint\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/pi0_libero_openpi\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    device = args.device\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    log.info(\"Loading policy on %s...\", device)\n    from openpi_metabrain_policy import OpenPIMetaBrainPolicy\n    policy_wrapper = OpenPIMetaBrainPolicy.from_checkpoint(\n        checkpoint_path=args.checkpoint,\n        routing_mode='none',\n        device=device,\n        use_delta_actions=True,\n    )\n    policy_wrapper._diag_max = 0\n\n    collector = Layer6Collector(policy_wrapper.model)\n    collector.register()\n\n    # Verify activation norm\n    log.info(\"Verifying hook with dummy forward pass...\")\n    from evaluate_libero_plus import obs_to_model_input\n    dummy_obs = {\n        \"agentview_image\": np.zeros((224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\n        \"robot0_eye_in_hand_image\": np.zeros((224, 224, 3), dtype=np.uint8),\n        \"robot0_eef_pos\": np.zeros(3, dtype=np.float32),\n        \"robot0_eef_quat\": np.array([0, 0, 0, 1], dtype=np.float32),\n        \"robot0_gripper_qpos\": np.zeros(2, dtype=np.float32),\n    }\n    dummy_dict = obs_to_model_input(dummy_obs, \"test task\")\n    _ = policy_wrapper.infer(dummy_dict)\n    test_act = collector.get()\n    if test_act is not None:\n        norm = test_act.norm().item()\n        log.info(\"Verification: activation dim=%d, norm=%.1f\", test_act.shape[-1], norm)\n        if norm > 10000:\n            log.error(\"ACTIVATION NORM ABNORMAL (%.1f >> 250). Hook may be wrong!\", norm)\n            return\n    else:\n        log.error(\"Hook returned None! Check hook registration.\")\n        return\n\n    # Collect from each perturbation type\n    perturbation_types = [\n        \"background_textures\", \"camera_viewpoints\", \"light_conditions\", \"sensor_noise\",\n        \"objects_layout\", \"robot_initial_states\",\n    ]\n    \n    # Balance classes: visual has 4 sub-types, spatial 1, physical 1\n    # Collect fewer per visual sub-type to balance\n    samples_config = {\n        \"background_textures\": args.num_tasks_per_perturbation,\n        \"camera_viewpoints\": args.num_tasks_per_perturbation,\n        \"light_conditions\": args.num_tasks_per_perturbation,\n        \"sensor_noise\": args.num_tasks_per_perturbation,\n        \"objects_layout\": args.num_tasks_per_perturbation * 2,\n        \"robot_initial_states\": args.num_tasks_per_perturbation * 2,\n    }\n\n    all_activations = []\n    all_metadata = []\n\n    for ptype in perturbation_types:\n        n_tasks = samples_config[ptype]\n        log.info(\"Collecting from %s (%d tasks × %d steps)...\", ptype, n_tasks, args.steps_per_task)\n        t0 = time.time()\n        \n        acts, meta = collect_from_perturbation(\n            policy_wrapper, collector, args.task_suite, ptype,\n            n_tasks, args.steps_per_task, args.seed\n        )\n        \n        elapsed = time.time() - t0\n        log.info(\"  %s: %d activations in %.1fs\", ptype, len(acts), elapsed)\n        all_activations.extend(acts)\n        all_metadata.extend(meta)\n\n    # Save results\n    if not all_activations:\n        log.error(\"No activations collected!\")\n        return\n\n    X = torch.stack(all_activations)\n    labels_3class = torch.tensor([CLASS_TO_IDX[m[\"class_3\"]] for m in all_metadata])\n    \n    # Summary\n    class_counts = collections.Counter(labels_3class.numpy().tolist())\n    log.info(\"Total samples: %d\", len(X))\n    log.info(\"Class distribution: visual=%d, spatial=%d, physical=%d\",\n             class_counts.get(0, 0), class_counts.get(1, 0), class_counts.get(2, 0))\n    log.info(\"Activation shape: %s, mean norm: %.1f\", X.shape, X.norm(dim=-1).mean().item())\n\n    save_path = Path(args.output_dir) / \"activations.pt\"\n    torch.save({\n        \"activations\": X,\n        \"labels_3class\": labels_3class,\n        \"metadata\": all_metadata,\n        \"config\": {\n            \"task_suite\": args.task_suite,\n            \"num_tasks_per_perturbation\": args.num_tasks_per_perturbation,\n            \"steps_per_task\": args.steps_per_task,\n            \"seed\": args.seed,\n            \"device\": device,\n            \"hook_path\": \"paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.model.language_model.layers.6\",\n            \"pooling\": \"first_token\",\n        }\n    }, save_path)\n    log.info(\"Saved to %s\", save_path)\n\n    policy_wrapper.cleanup()\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPT\necho \"Script written successfully\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2371,"cache_read_input_tokens":54030,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":2371},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"cbea082d-eac9-4f77-9b36-638b6bf0cf28","uuid":"7bce2d01-bf72-464d-9013-3930e131ca0f"}

\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\nPAR Router inference module.\\\\n\\\\nLoads a trained PAR checkpoint and provides routing weights\\\\nfor Meta-Brain LoRA expert selection.\\\\n\\\\nUsage:\\\\n    from phase1.par_router import PARRouter\\\\n\\\\n    router = PARRouter.from_checkpoint(\\\\\\\"checkpoints/phase1/par_router/best_router.pt\\\\\\\")\\\\n    weights = router.route(hidden_states)  # [B, 3] softmax weights\\\\n\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\nimport torch\\\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\\\nfrom typing import Optional\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nCLASS_3_NAMES = [\\\\\\\"visual\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"spatial\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"physical\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass PARRouterModel(nn.Module):\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"2-layer MLP: hidden_states → 3-class logits.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, input_dim: int = 2048, hidden_dim: int = 256,\\\\n                 num_classes: int = 3, dropout: float = 0.1):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\\\\n            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim),\\\\n            nn.ReLU(),\\\\n            nn.Dropout(dropout),\\\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, num_classes),\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        return self.net(x)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass PARRouter:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"High-level router that wraps checkpoint loading and inference.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, model: PARRouterModel, config: dict,\\\\n                 device: torch.device = torch.device(\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\")):\\\\n        self.model = model.to(device).eval()\\\\n        self.config = config\\\\n        self.device = device\\\\n        self.layer = config.get(\\\\\\\"layer\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"paligemma_layer_6\\\\\\\")\\\\n        self.pooling = config.get(\\\\\\\"pooling\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"first\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    @classmethod\\\\n    def from_checkpoint(cls, checkpoint_path: str,\\\\n                        device: Optional[torch.device] = None) -> \\\\\\\"PARRouter\\\\\\\":\\\\n        if device is None:\\\\n            device = torch.device(\\\\\\\"cuda\\\\\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        ckpt = torch.load(checkpoint_path, map_location=device, weights_only=False)\\\\n        config = ckpt[\\\\\\\"config\\\\\\\"]\\\\n\\\\n        model = PARRouterModel(\\\\n            input_dim=config[\\\\\\\"input_dim\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            hidden_dim=config[\\\\\\\"hidden_dim\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            num_classes=config[\\\\\\\"num_classes\\\\\\\"],\\\\n            dropout=config.get(\\\\\\\"dropout\\\\\\\", 0.1),\\\\n        )\\\\n        model.load_state_dict(ckpt[\\\\\\\"model_state_dict\\\\\\\"])\\\\n\\\\n        return cls(model=model, config=config, device=device)\\\\n\\\\n    def _pool(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Pool 3D hidden states [B, seq_len, D] → [B, D].\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if x.dim() == 2:\\\\n            return x\\\\n        if self.pooling == \\\\\\\"first\\\\\\\":\\\\n            return x[:, 0, :]\\\\n        elif self.pooling == \\\\\\\"last\\\\\\\":\\\\n            return x[:, -1, :]\\\\n        elif self.pooling == \\\\\\\"first_last\\\\\\\":\\\\n            return (x[:, 0, :] + x[:, -1, :]) / 2\\\\n        else:  # mean\\\\n            return x.mean(dim=1)\\\\n\\\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\\\n    def route(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Compute soft routing weights from hidden states.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            hidden_states: [B, seq_len, D] or [B, D] (pre-pooled).\\\\n\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            [B, 3] softmax routing weights: p(visual), p(spatial), p(physical).\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        x = hidden_states.to(self.device).float()\\\\n        x = self._pool(x)\\\\n        logits = self.model(x)\\\\n        return torch.softmax(logits, dim=-1)\\\\n\\\\n    def route_argmax(self, hidden_states: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, list[str]]:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Hard routing: return class index and name.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        weights = self.route(hidden_states)\\\\n        indices = weights.argmax(dim=-1)\\\\n        names = [CLASS_3_NAMES[i] for i in indices.cpu().tolist()]\\\\n        return indices, names\\\\n\\\\n    def __repr__(self) -> str:\\\\n        return (f\\\\\\\"PARRouter(layer={self.layer}, pooling={self.pooling}, \\\\\\\"\\\\n                f\\\\\\\"device={self.device})\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\", \\\"stderr\\\": \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"2-layer MLP: hidden_states → 3-class logits.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, input_dim: int = 2048, hidden_dim: int = 256,\\\\n                 num_classes: int = 3, dropout: float = 0.1):\\\\n        super().__init__()\\\\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\\\\n            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim),\\\\n            nn.ReLU(),\\\\n            nn.Dropout(dropout),\\\\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, num_classes),\\\\n        )\\\\n\\\\n    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        return self.net(x)\\\\n\\\\n\\\\nclass PARRouter:\\\\n    \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"High-level router that wraps checkpoint loading and inference.\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n\\\\n    def __init__(self, model: PARRouterModel, config: dict,\\\\n                 device: torch.device = torch.device(\\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\")):\\\\n        self.model = model.to(device).eval()\\\\n        self.config = config\\\\n        self.device = device\\\\n        self.layer = config.get(\\\\\\\"layer\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"paligemma_layer_6\\\\\\\")\\\\n        self.pooling = config.get(\\\\\\\"pooling\\\\\\\", \\\\\\\"first\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n    @classmethod\\\\n    def from_checkpoint(cls, checkpoint_path: str,\\\\n                        device: Optional[torch.device] = None) -> \\\\\\\"PARRouter\\\\\\\":\\\\n        if device is None:\\\\n            device = torch.device(\\\\\\\"cuda\\\\\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\\\\\"cpu\\\\\\\")\\\\n\\\\n        ckpt = dropout=config.get(\\\\\\\"dropout\\\\\\\", 0.1),\\\\n        )\\\\n        model.load_state_dict(ckpt[\\\\\\\"model_state_dict\\\\\\\"])\\\\n\\\\n        return cls(model=model, config=config, device=device)\\\\n\\\\n    def _pool(self, x: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:\\\\n        \\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"Pool 3D hidden states [B, seq_len, D] → [B, D].\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\\\\"\\\\n        if x.dim() == 2:\\\\n            return x\\\\n        if self.pooling == \\\\\\\"first\\\\\\\":\\\\n            return x[:, 0, :]\\\\n        elif self.pooling == \\\\\\\"last\\\\\\\":\\\\n            return x[:, -1, :]\\\\n        elif self.pooling == \\\\\\\"first_last\\\\\\\":\\\\n            return (x[:, 0, :] + x[:, -1, :]) / 2\\\\n        else:  # mean\\\\n            return states.\\\\n\\\\n        Args:\\\\n            hidden_states: [B, seq_len, D] or [B, D] (pre-pooled).\\\\n\\\\n        Returns:\\\\n            [B, 3] softmax routing weights: p(visual), p(spatial), {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01H1ivbkAdYaK9m2yBtQ5Mhn","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01EYsRGLi77qUYw7LNKqa3nM","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-34142","command":"cat > /root/meta_brain_lora_routing/phase0/train_router_real_env.py << 'SCRIPT'\n\"\"\"\nTrain PAR Router on real LIBERO-Plus activations (3-class: visual/spatial/physical).\n\nInput: activations.pt from collect_activations_real_env.py\nOutput: PAR router checkpoint compatible with phase1/par_router.py\n\n5-fold stratified cross-validation, reports macro F1.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport argparse\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nimport torch.nn as nn\nimport torch.optim as optim\nfrom sklearn.metrics import f1_score, classification_report\nfrom sklearn.model_selection import StratifiedKFold\nfrom torch.utils.data import DataLoader, TensorDataset, Subset\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\")\nlog = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nCLASS_NAMES = [\"visual\", \"spatial\", \"physical\"]\n\n\nclass PARRouterModel(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, input_dim=2048, hidden_dim=256, num_classes=3, dropout=0.1):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.net = nn.Sequential(\n            nn.Linear(input_dim, hidden_dim),\n            nn.ReLU(),\n            nn.Dropout(dropout),\n            nn.Linear(hidden_dim, num_classes),\n        )\n\n    def forward(self, x):\n        return self.net(x)\n\n\ndef train_one_fold(X_train, y_train, X_val, y_val, input_dim, num_classes,\n                   lr, epochs, device, hidden_dim=256, dropout=0.1):\n    model = PARRouterModel(input_dim, hidden_dim, num_classes, dropout).to(device)\n    optimizer = optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=lr, weight_decay=1e-4)\n    scheduler = optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(optimizer, T_max=epochs)\n    criterion = nn.CrossEntropyLoss()\n\n    train_ds = TensorDataset(X_train.to(device), y_train.to(device))\n    train_loader = DataLoader(train_ds, batch_size=128, shuffle=True)\n\n    best_f1 = 0.0\n    best_state = None\n\n    for epoch in range(epochs):\n        model.train()\n        for xb, yb in train_loader:\n            optimizer.zero_grad()\n            loss = criterion(model(xb), yb)\n            loss.backward()\n            optimizer.step()\n        scheduler.step()\n\n        model.eval()\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            logits = model(X_val.to(device))\n            preds = logits.argmax(dim=-1).cpu().numpy()\n            y_true = y_val.numpy()\n            f1 = f1_score(y_true, preds, average=\"macro\")\n\n        if f1 > best_f1:\n            best_f1 = f1\n            best_state = {k: v.cpu().clone() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}\n\n    return best_f1, best_state\n\n\ndef cross_validate(X, y, input_dim, num_classes, lr, epochs, device,\n                   n_folds=5, hidden_dim=256, dropout=0.1):\n    skf = StratifiedKFold(n_splits=n_folds, shuffle=True, random_state=42)\n    fold_f1s = []\n    best_overall_f1 = 0.0\n    best_overall_state = None\n\n    for fold, (train_idx, val_idx) in enumerate(skf.split(X, y)):\n        X_train, X_val = X[train_idx], X[val_idx]\n        y_train, y_val = y[train_idx], y[val_idx]\n\n        f1, state = train_one_fold(\n            X_train, y_train, X_val, y_val,\n            input_dim, num_classes, lr, epochs, device, hidden_dim, dropout\n        )\n        fold_f1s.append(f1)\n        log.info(\"  Fold %d: F1=%.4f\", fold + 1, f1)\n\n        if f1 > best_overall_f1:\n            best_overall_f1 = f1\n            best_overall_state = state\n\n    mean_f1 = np.mean(fold_f1s)\n    std_f1 = np.std(fold_f1s)\n    log.info(\"  CV: F1=%.4f +/- %.4f\", mean_f1, std_f1)\n    return mean_f1, std_f1, fold_f1s, best_overall_state\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()\n    parser.add_argument(\"--data_path\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/activations_real_env/activations.pt\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--output_dir\", default=\"/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/par_router_real_env\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--device\", default=\"cuda:2\")\n    parser.add_argument(\"--lr\", type=float, default=1e-3)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--epochs\", type=int, default=100)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--hidden_dim\", type=int, default=256)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--dropout\", type=float, default=0.1)\n    parser.add_argument(\"--normalize\", action=\"store_true\")\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    device = args.device\n    os.makedirs(args.output_dir, exist_ok=True)\n\n    log.info(\"Loading activations from %s\", args.data_path)\n    data = torch.load(args.data_path, weights_only=False)\n    X = data[\"activations\"]\n    y = data[\"labels_3class\"]\n\n    log.info(\"Data: X=%s, y=%s\", X.shape, y.shape)\n    log.info(\"Class counts: %s\", {CLASS_NAMES[i]: (y == i).sum().item() for i in range(3)})\n    log.info(\"Mean activation norm: %.1f\", X.norm(dim=-1).mean().item())\n\n    if args.normalize:\n        log.info(\"Applying L2 normalization\")\n        X = torch.nn.functional.normalize(X, dim=-1)\n\n    input_dim = X.shape[-1]\n    num_classes = 3\n\n    # Try multiple learning rates\n    lrs = [1e-4, 5e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3]\n    best_result = None\n\n    for lr in lrs:\n        log.info(\"Training with lr=%.1e\", lr)\n        mean_f1, std_f1, fold_f1s, best_state = cross_validate(\n            X, y, input_dim, num_classes, lr, args.epochs, device,\n            hidden_dim=args.hidden_dim, dropout=args.dropout\n        )\n        if best_result is None or mean_f1 > best_result[\"mean_f1\"]:\n            best_result = {\n                \"mean_f1\": mean_f1,\n                \"std_f1\": std_f1,\n                \"fold_f1s\": fold_f1s,\n                \"lr\": lr,\n                \"state\": best_state,\n            }\n\n    log.info(\"Best: lr=%.1e, F1=%.4f +/- %.4f\", \n             best_result[\"lr\"], best_result[\"mean_f1\"], best_result[\"std_f1\"])\n\n    # Save checkpoint (compatible with par_router.py)\n    config = {\n        \"input_dim\": input_dim,\n        \"hidden_dim\": args.hidden_dim,\n        \"num_classes\": num_classes,\n        \"dropout\": args.dropout,\n        \"layer\": \"paligemma_layer_6\",\n        \"pooling\": \"first\",\n        \"lr\": best_result[\"lr\"],\n        \"normalize\": args.normalize,\n    }\n\n    ckpt_path = Path(args.output_dir) / \"best_router.pt\"\n    torch.save({\n        \"model_state_dict\": best_result[\"state\"],\n        \"config\": config,\n        \"cv_results\": {\n            \"mean_f1\": best_result[\"mean_f1\"],\n            \"std_f1\": best_result[\"std_f1\"],\n            \"fold_f1s\": best_result[\"fold_f1s\"],\n        },\n        \"data_info\": {\n            \"source\": str(args.data_path),\n            \"num_samples\": len(X),\n            \"class_counts\": {CLASS_NAMES[i]: (y == i).sum().item() for i in range(3)},\n        }\n    }, ckpt_path)\n    log.info(\"Router checkpoint saved to %s\", ckpt_path)\n\n    # Also save result JSON\n    result_path = Path(args.output_dir) / \"training_result.json\"\n    with open(result_path, \"w\") as f:\n        json.dump({\n            \"mean_f1\": best_result[\"mean_f1\"],\n            \"std_f1\": best_result[\"std_f1\"],\n            \"fold_f1s\": best_result[\"fold_f1s\"],\n            \"best_lr\": best_result[\"lr\"],\n            \"config\": config,\n            \"data_info\": {\n                \"source\": str(args.data_path),\n                \"num_samples\": len(X),\n                \"class_counts\": {CLASS_NAMES[i]: (y == i).sum().item() for i in range(3)},\n            }\n        }, f, indent=2)\n    log.info(\"Result saved to %s\", result_path)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\nSCRIPT\necho \"Router training script written\"","account":"root"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1583,"cache_read_input_tokens":64808,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1583},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"cbea082d-eac9-4f77-9b36-638b6bf0cf28","uuid":"c37715ff-fcc0-4a08-a867-9afc4f37321a"}
